# Scalar scripted triggers only; no parameter substitution.
# Preserve native activation for roles without an original visible block.
RUS_ui_use_faction_advisor_filters = { original_tag = RUS is_ai = no OR = { has_government = totalist has_government = communist has_government = syndicalist has_government = radical_socialist } }
RUS_ui_advisor_matches_faction_RUS_aleksandr_dikgof_derental = {
 OR = { NOT = { RUS_ui_use_faction_advisor_filters = yes }
  NOT = { OR = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_bolshevik has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_populist has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_maximalist has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_left has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_centre has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_right has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_menshevik has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sds } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_bolshevik RUS_aleksandr_dikgof_derental = { OR = { has_trait = KR_bolshevik_bukharin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_frunze has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kaganovich has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kalinin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kamenev has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kirov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_stalin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_supreme_soviet has_trait = KR_bolshevik_sverdlov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_yezhov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_zhdanov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_zinovyev } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr RUS_aleksandr_dikgof_derental = { OR = { has_trait = KR_sr_blyumkin has_trait = KR_sr_sattel has_trait = KR_sr_steinberg } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_populist RUS_aleksandr_dikgof_derental = { OR = { has_trait = KR_spiridonist_elyashevich has_trait = KR_spiridonist_kamkov has_trait = KR_spiridonist_karelin has_trait = KR_spiridonist_mayorov has_trait = KR_spiridonist_zdobnov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_maximalist RUS_aleksandr_dikgof_derental = { OR = { has_trait = KR_maximalist_gronsky has_trait = KR_maximalist_kakhovskaya has_trait = KR_maximalist_kolegayev has_trait = KR_maximalist_ustinov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_left RUS_aleksandr_dikgof_derental = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_left_frunze has_trait = KR_vst_left_gronsky has_trait = KR_vst_left_kaganovich has_trait = KR_vst_left_kamenev has_trait = KR_vst_left_medvedev has_trait = KR_vst_left_syrtsov has_trait = KR_vst_left_zinovyev } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_centre RUS_aleksandr_dikgof_derental = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_centre_kollontay has_trait = KR_vst_centre_levitsky has_trait = KR_vst_centre_maksimov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_shatov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_shlyapnikov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_strumilin has_trait = KR_vst_centre_sulimov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_tomsky } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_right RUS_aleksandr_dikgof_derental = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_right_abramovich has_trait = KR_vst_right_dan has_trait = KR_vst_right_finn_yenotayevsky has_trait = KR_vst_right_kirov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_menshevik RUS_aleksandr_dikgof_derental = { OR = { has_trait = KR_menshevik_doctor_sic has_trait = KR_menshevik_grandfather_sic } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sds RUS_aleksandr_dikgof_derental = { OR = { has_trait = KR_sds_zeller } } }
 }
}

  NOT = { OR = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_bolshevik has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_populist has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_maximalist has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_left has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_centre has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_right has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_menshevik has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sds } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_bolshevik RUS_grigory_maksimov = { OR = { has_trait = KR_bolshevik_bukharin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_frunze has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kaganovich has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kalinin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kamenev has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kirov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_stalin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_supreme_soviet has_trait = KR_bolshevik_sverdlov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_yezhov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_zhdanov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_zinovyev } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr RUS_grigory_maksimov = { OR = { has_trait = KR_sr_blyumkin has_trait = KR_sr_sattel has_trait = KR_sr_steinberg } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_populist RUS_grigory_maksimov = { OR = { has_trait = KR_spiridonist_elyashevich has_trait = KR_spiridonist_kamkov has_trait = KR_spiridonist_karelin has_trait = KR_spiridonist_mayorov has_trait = KR_spiridonist_zdobnov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_maximalist RUS_grigory_maksimov = { OR = { has_trait = KR_maximalist_gronsky has_trait = KR_maximalist_kakhovskaya has_trait = KR_maximalist_kolegayev has_trait = KR_maximalist_ustinov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_left RUS_grigory_maksimov = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_left_frunze has_trait = KR_vst_left_gronsky has_trait = KR_vst_left_kaganovich has_trait = KR_vst_left_kamenev has_trait = KR_vst_left_medvedev has_trait = KR_vst_left_syrtsov has_trait = KR_vst_left_zinovyev } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_centre RUS_grigory_maksimov = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_centre_kollontay has_trait = KR_vst_centre_levitsky has_trait = KR_vst_centre_maksimov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_shatov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_shlyapnikov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_strumilin has_trait = KR_vst_centre_sulimov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_tomsky } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_right RUS_grigory_maksimov = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_right_abramovich has_trait = KR_vst_right_dan has_trait = KR_vst_right_finn_yenotayevsky has_trait = KR_vst_right_kirov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_menshevik RUS_grigory_maksimov = { OR = { has_trait = KR_menshevik_doctor_sic has_trait = KR_menshevik_grandfather_sic } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sds RUS_grigory_maksimov = { OR = { has_trait = KR_sds_zeller } } }
 }
}

  NOT = { OR = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_bolshevik has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_populist has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_maximalist has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_left has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_centre has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_right has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_menshevik has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sds } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_bolshevik RUS_isaak_steinberg = { OR = { has_trait = KR_bolshevik_bukharin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_frunze has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kaganovich has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kalinin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kamenev has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kirov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_stalin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_supreme_soviet has_trait = KR_bolshevik_sverdlov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_yezhov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_zhdanov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_zinovyev } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr RUS_isaak_steinberg = { OR = { has_trait = KR_sr_blyumkin has_trait = KR_sr_sattel has_trait = KR_sr_steinberg } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_populist RUS_isaak_steinberg = { OR = { has_trait = KR_spiridonist_elyashevich has_trait = KR_spiridonist_kamkov has_trait = KR_spiridonist_karelin has_trait = KR_spiridonist_mayorov has_trait = KR_spiridonist_zdobnov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_maximalist RUS_isaak_steinberg = { OR = { has_trait = KR_maximalist_gronsky has_trait = KR_maximalist_kakhovskaya has_trait = KR_maximalist_kolegayev has_trait = KR_maximalist_ustinov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_left RUS_isaak_steinberg = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_left_frunze has_trait = KR_vst_left_gronsky has_trait = KR_vst_left_kaganovich has_trait = KR_vst_left_kamenev has_trait = KR_vst_left_medvedev has_trait = KR_vst_left_syrtsov has_trait = KR_vst_left_zinovyev } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_centre RUS_isaak_steinberg = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_centre_kollontay has_trait = KR_vst_centre_levitsky has_trait = KR_vst_centre_maksimov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_shatov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_shlyapnikov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_strumilin has_trait = KR_vst_centre_sulimov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_tomsky } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_right RUS_isaak_steinberg = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_right_abramovich has_trait = KR_vst_right_dan has_trait = KR_vst_right_finn_yenotayevsky has_trait = KR_vst_right_kirov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_menshevik RUS_isaak_steinberg = { OR = { has_trait = KR_menshevik_doctor_sic has_trait = KR_menshevik_grandfather_sic } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sds RUS_isaak_steinberg = { OR = { has_trait = KR_sds_zeller } } }
 }

  NOT = { OR = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_bolshevik has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_populist has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_maximalist has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_left has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_centre has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_right has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_menshevik has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sds } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_bolshevik RUS_kliment_voroshilov = { OR = { has_trait = KR_bolshevik_bukharin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_frunze has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kaganovich has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kalinin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kamenev has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kirov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_stalin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_supreme_soviet has_trait = KR_bolshevik_sverdlov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_yezhov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_zhdanov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_zinovyev } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr RUS_kliment_voroshilov = { OR = { has_trait = KR_sr_blyumkin has_trait = KR_sr_sattel has_trait = KR_sr_steinberg } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_populist RUS_kliment_voroshilov = { OR = { has_trait = KR_spiridonist_elyashevich has_trait = KR_spiridonist_kamkov has_trait = KR_spiridonist_karelin has_trait = KR_spiridonist_mayorov has_trait = KR_spiridonist_zdobnov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_maximalist RUS_kliment_voroshilov = { OR = { has_trait = KR_maximalist_gronsky has_trait = KR_maximalist_kakhovskaya has_trait = KR_maximalist_kolegayev has_trait = KR_maximalist_ustinov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_left RUS_kliment_voroshilov = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_left_frunze has_trait = KR_vst_left_gronsky has_trait = KR_vst_left_kaganovich has_trait = KR_vst_left_kamenev has_trait = KR_vst_left_medvedev has_trait = KR_vst_left_syrtsov has_trait = KR_vst_left_zinovyev } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_centre RUS_kliment_voroshilov = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_centre_kollontay has_trait = KR_vst_centre_levitsky has_trait = KR_vst_centre_maksimov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_shatov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_shlyapnikov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_strumilin has_trait = KR_vst_centre_sulimov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_tomsky } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_right RUS_kliment_voroshilov = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_right_abramovich has_trait = KR_vst_right_dan has_trait = KR_vst_right_finn_yenotayevsky has_trait = KR_vst_right_kirov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_menshevik RUS_kliment_voroshilov = { OR = { has_trait = KR_menshevik_doctor_sic has_trait = KR_menshevik_grandfather_sic } } }

  NOT = { OR = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_bolshevik has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_populist has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_maximalist has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_left has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_centre has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_right has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_menshevik has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sds } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_bolshevik RUS_maria_zakharchenko = { OR = { has_trait = KR_bolshevik_bukharin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_frunze has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kaganovich has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kalinin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kamenev has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kirov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_stalin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_supreme_soviet has_trait = KR_bolshevik_sverdlov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_yezhov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_zhdanov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_zinovyev } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr RUS_maria_zakharchenko = { OR = { has_trait = KR_sr_blyumkin has_trait = KR_sr_sattel has_trait = KR_sr_steinberg } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_populist RUS_maria_zakharchenko = { OR = { has_trait = KR_spiridonist_elyashevich has_trait = KR_spiridonist_kamkov has_trait = KR_spiridonist_karelin has_trait = KR_spiridonist_mayorov has_trait = KR_spiridonist_zdobnov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_maximalist RUS_maria_zakharchenko = { OR = { has_trait = KR_maximalist_gronsky has_trait = KR_maximalist_kakhovskaya has_trait = KR_maximalist_kolegayev has_trait = KR_maximalist_ustinov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_left RUS_maria_zakharchenko = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_left_frunze has_trait = KR_vst_left_gronsky has_trait = KR_vst_left_kaganovich has_trait = KR_vst_left_kamenev has_trait = KR_vst_left_medvedev has_trait = KR_vst_left_syrtsov has_trait = KR_vst_left_zinovyev } } }

  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_right RUS_maria_zakharchenko = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_right_abramovich has_trait = KR_vst_right_dan has_trait = KR_vst_right_finn_yenotayevsky has_trait = KR_vst_right_kirov } } }

  NOT = { OR = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_bolshevik has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_populist has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_maximalist has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_left has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_centre has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_right has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_menshevik has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sds } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_bolshevik RUS_nikolay_kondratyev = { OR = { has_trait = KR_bolshevik_bukharin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_frunze has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kaganovich has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kalinin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kamenev has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kirov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_stalin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_supreme_soviet has_trait = KR_bolshevik_sverdlov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_yezhov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_zhdanov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_zinovyev } } }

  NOT = { OR = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_bolshevik has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_populist has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_maximalist has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_left has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_centre has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_right has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_menshevik has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sds } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_bolshevik RUS_nikolay_trubetskoy = { OR = { has_trait = KR_bolshevik_bukharin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_frunze has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kaganovich has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kalinin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kamenev has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kirov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_stalin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_supreme_soviet has_trait = KR_bolshevik_sverdlov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_yezhov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_zhdanov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_zinovyev } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr RUS_nikolay_trubetskoy = { OR = { has_trait = KR_sr_blyumkin has_trait = KR_sr_sattel has_trait = KR_sr_steinberg } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_populist RUS_nikolay_trubetskoy = { OR = { has_trait = KR_spiridonist_elyashevich has_trait = KR_spiridonist_kamkov has_trait = KR_spiridonist_karelin has_trait = KR_spiridonist_mayorov has_trait = KR_spiridonist_zdobnov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_maximalist RUS_nikolay_trubetskoy = { OR = { has_trait = KR_maximalist_gronsky has_trait = KR_maximalist_kakhovskaya has_trait = KR_maximalist_kolegayev has_trait = KR_maximalist_ustinov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_left RUS_nikolay_trubetskoy = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_left_frunze has_trait = KR_vst_left_gronsky has_trait = KR_vst_left_kaganovich has_trait = KR_vst_left_kamenev has_trait = KR_vst_left_medvedev has_trait = KR_vst_left_syrtsov has_trait = KR_vst_left_zinovyev } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_centre RUS_nikolay_trubetskoy = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_centre_kollontay has_trait = KR_vst_centre_levitsky has_trait = KR_vst_centre_maksimov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_shatov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_shlyapnikov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_strumilin has_trait = KR_vst_centre_sulimov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_tomsky } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_right RUS_nikolay_trubetskoy = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_right_abramovich has_trait = KR_vst_right_dan has_trait = KR_vst_right_finn_yenotayevsky has_trait = KR_vst_right_kirov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_menshevik RUS_nikolay_trubetskoy = { OR = { has_trait = KR_menshevik_doctor_sic has_trait = KR_menshevik_grandfather_sic } } }

  NOT = { OR = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_bolshevik has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_populist has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_maximalist has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_left has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_centre has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_right has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_menshevik has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sds } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_bolshevik RUS_pavel_gronsky = { OR = { has_trait = KR_bolshevik_bukharin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_frunze has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kaganovich has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kalinin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kamenev has_trait = KR_bolshevik_kirov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_stalin has_trait = KR_bolshevik_supreme_soviet has_trait = KR_bolshevik_sverdlov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_yezhov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_zhdanov has_trait = KR_bolshevik_zinovyev } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr RUS_pavel_gronsky = { OR = { has_trait = KR_sr_blyumkin has_trait = KR_sr_sattel has_trait = KR_sr_steinberg } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_populist RUS_pavel_gronsky = { OR = { has_trait = KR_spiridonist_elyashevich has_trait = KR_spiridonist_kamkov has_trait = KR_spiridonist_karelin has_trait = KR_spiridonist_mayorov has_trait = KR_spiridonist_zdobnov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sr_maximalist RUS_pavel_gronsky = { OR = { has_trait = KR_maximalist_gronsky has_trait = KR_maximalist_kakhovskaya has_trait = KR_maximalist_kolegayev has_trait = KR_maximalist_ustinov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_left RUS_pavel_gronsky = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_left_frunze has_trait = KR_vst_left_gronsky has_trait = KR_vst_left_kaganovich has_trait = KR_vst_left_kamenev has_trait = KR_vst_left_medvedev has_trait = KR_vst_left_syrtsov has_trait = KR_vst_left_zinovyev } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_centre RUS_pavel_gronsky = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_centre_kollontay has_trait = KR_vst_centre_levitsky has_trait = KR_vst_centre_maksimov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_shatov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_shlyapnikov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_strumilin has_trait = KR_vst_centre_sulimov has_trait = KR_vst_centre_tomsky } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_vst_right RUS_pavel_gronsky = { OR = { has_trait = KR_vst_right_abramovich has_trait = KR_vst_right_dan has_trait = KR_vst_right_finn_yenotayevsky has_trait = KR_vst_right_kirov } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_menshevik RUS_pavel_gronsky = { OR = { has_trait = KR_menshevik_doctor_sic has_trait = KR_menshevik_grandfather_sic } } }
  AND = { has_country_flag = RUS_ui_advisor_faction_sds RUS_pavel_gronsky = { OR = { has_trait = KR_sds_zeller } } }
 }
